# Migrate Your Performance or Partner Program onto impact.com | Other | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom.md)
.

When migrating to impact.com, you'll get access to an expert team dedicated to setting you up efficiently and successfully. If you're looking to learn more about what it would look like to move your program over to impact.com, email `sales@impact.com` for a no-strings-attached demo. Below is a guide to the migration process and best practices.

#### Migration Roles & responsibilities[](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#migration-roles-and-responsibilities)

Role

Responsibilities

**Affiliate Manager or Partner Manager**

This role manages the migration of partners from the old platform, to impact.com, with help from the Onboarding Migration Specialist.

**Agency Account or Project Manager**

If you've engaged with an agency, a representative from the agency usually manages the partner migration in your stead and acts as the Project Manager.

**impact.com Onboarding Success Manager**

This role owns the new account setup process and makes sure that all aspects of the new program are configured correctly, including tracking settings, product catalogs, and partner contracts.

**impact.com Implementation Engineer**

This role owns the technical integration process, liaising directly with your tech team as necessary to make sure that the technical aspects of tracking are working flawlessly.

**Web Developer or Technical Contact**

This role is responsible for placing JavaScript across your site, assisting with any necessary data transfers, testing integrations, and making sure the technical project is completed and tested in a timely manner.

#### The migration process[](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#the-migration-process)

impact.com typically recommends notifying your partners about the transition as soon as possible. It is also recommended to have a 2-4 week overlap between closing down your old program and running the new program, to allow time for partners to sign up and switch out links.

Once impact.com gets the green light to begin migration, the following steps take place.

1.  **Account setup**
    
    At this stage, you'll receive a designated impact.com Onboarding Success Manager, who will work closely with you to configure all account settings, including tracking, contracts, and product catalogs.
    
2.  **Technical account integration**
    
    At the same time, an impact.com Implementation Engineer will manage the technical integration process, working with the web developer or technical contact to ensure all tracking works, all parameters are passed correctly, and everything is tested thoroughly.
    
3.  **Partner migration**
    
    Now the program is ready to go from a technical standpoint, and partners can be brought over and set live within the app.
    
    Your affiliate or partner manager (or your agency) will communicate with your partner base about the upcoming migration. The impact.com Onboarding Migration Specialist will then provide best practices and messaging templates for migrating your partner base and will check in frequently to ensure a smooth migration.
    
    *   **If you have Optimize included:** It's best to migrate partners one group at a time, rather than all at once. impact.com ensures conversion [deduplication](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/cross-channel-performance-insights/configure-affiliate-networks-for-deduplication)
         while both platforms are active.
        
    

And with that the migration is complete.

#### Best practices[](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#best-practices)

*   Communicate with your existing Network advising them that you are moving off and will not be utilizing their services any longer.
    
    **Important:** Check your cancellation policy with your current network first as there may be clauses in your contract that could prevent migration.
    
*   Plan on a realistic timeline switch to facilitate the move and allow partners adequate time to make or schedule changes.
    
*   Take into account the needs of partners as some partners have integration needs and will therefore need to be communicated in advance.
    
*   Ensure that partner payments on the current network are up to date as partners sometimes do not migrate due to outstanding owing payments.
    
*   Keep partners in the know as developments around the Migration unfold. Where possible, provide an email alias to filter any questions ahead of the move. You can also create a guide or [offer resources to your partners](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/ads/requests-from-partners/create-a-resource-to-share-with-partners)
     who are newly migrated to impact.com. Have a look under ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3964917321-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FhRN1rcrim887TwHLBjac%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0e364ff97d54f734964f43df8ebaef76&sv=3) **Engage → Content → Resources** for more helpful migration information.
    

#### Migration communications[](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#migration-communications)

The communication timeline below allows 6-8 weeks for a full migration but this period can be shorter or longer, depending on the volume of partners:

8 Week timeline[](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#id-8-week-timeline)

2 weeks before launch date

A notification of upcoming migration gets sent which includes the end date and additional information regarding impact.com. Calls should be scheduled with all top-tier partners to personally notify them and get them to switch out links as soon as they become available (after launch). The priority is to get all top-tier partners migrated over. See the **Pre-Launch Email Template** below.

impact.com recommends downloading a full partner list before getting started. This could be helpful in cases where the partner doesn't migrate in time.

A performance report from your prior network/platform can help prioritize the migration of sale-generating partners. This will serve as your roadmap to limit a drop in sales revenue that can happen during a migration.

Launch day

Send an email to all partners including the direct sign-up link for the Public Terms or specific template term sign-up links (depending on the partner). Reiterate the end date of the old program.

1 week after launch

Top partners should have been contacted and should be in the process of switching out links. impact.com recommends monitoring their migration closely. See the **Post-Launch Email Template** below.

2 weeks after launch

Begin to prioritize outreach to mid-tier partners. Send out another communication reminding publishers that sales that occur after the end date will not be credited on the old platform. If they would like to continue promoting the campaign, they will need to join your program on impact.com.

2 weeks before migration ends

impact.com will start to scrape existing partner sites looking for the link of legacy networks. These partners can then be added to a communication workflow in impact.com and nurtured to update the outdated links.

Prioritize follow-up with your top-tier partners that are still pending.

1 week before migration ends

Send a reminder email that the program will be shutting down in 7 days, including the link to your impact.com account sign-up page. Ideally, all top-tier and the majority of mid-tier partners should be live on impact.com by this point.

1 day before migration ends

Send final communication to remind partners that any sales after the migration end date will NOT be valid, and include the sign-up link to impact.com.

Migration completed

All top-tier partners have signed up and are tracking through their impact.com links. All mid and lower-tier partners who have not signed up will still be able to do so through the marketplace or wherever your sign-up link is hosted.

There are two sample emails you can send to your existing partners to announce the upcoming migration to impact.com.

**Deluxe migration package only**: Customized messaging can be discussed with the Migration Specialist.

Sample 1: Pre-launch email

Sample 2: Post-launch email

This email can be sent to your existing partners to announce the upcoming migration to impact.com.

**Subject**: ACTION REQUIRED: **Program Name** - Update

Hello `{first name}`,

We have very exciting news regarding our partner program! As of `{date links will expire}`, `{ProgramName}` will be running entirely on a more robust platform, and discontinuing our relationship with `{PreviousPlatform}`.

This change will enable us to provide more opportunities to our partners in terms of more accurate tracking, robust crediting logic, enhanced reporting capabilities, dynamic commissioning, as well as various new ad types. The new partner interface will also enable you to track your performance and payment status.

We will be sending a communication out shortly with additional details as well as signup information for our new tracking solution. If you don’t receive this communication within the next `{add ETA}` please reach out to me directly at `{contact’s email}`.

We appreciate your partnership and flexibility as we go through this change to provide a more scalable solution to our partner program.

Thank you,

This email can be sent to your partners to start the migration process.

**Subject**: ACTION REQUIRED: **Program Name** - Update

Hello `{first name}`,

As we’ve mentioned previously, the `{ProgramName}` partner program is transitioning from `{PreviousPlatform}` to impact.com. The last day our program will be available in `{PreviousPlatform}` is `{add date}`. Please transition your links before that time to ensure we are able to continue paying you commission.

To do this, please follow the below steps:

1.  Join the `{ProgramName}` program here `{add your impact.com sign-up link}`. You will receive an email once you are successfully signed up to our new program.
    
    *   [Log into](https://app.impact.com/login.user)
         your impact.com account.
        
    *   **Create** a link using the _Create and Share Link_ widget on your dashboard page.
        
    
2.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3964917321-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FhRN1rcrim887TwHLBjac%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c96dfc646871077ddf2e4cc2ea81175daa3d0531%252F79e45d44813a3f6d7b87329fec56d9ef69c7b18ad8c329528af6456767aaabac.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e5030b3b6b33b7967adffda49121d30c&sv=3) **\[Help\]** in the bottom right corner to access the impact.com Help Center or log a ticket with the support team.
    

We are here to answer questions and help you with the transition process as needed.

Please email us as soon as you've migrated!

Thank you,

[PreviousSupported Currencies & Timezones](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/supported-currencies-and-timezones)

# 将你的 Performance 或 Partner Program 迁移到 impact.com | Other | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/other/zh/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom.md)
.

在迁移到 impact.com 时，您将获得一个专门的专家团队，致力于高效且成功地为您完成设置。如果您想进一步了解将您的项目迁移到 impact.com 会是什么样子，请发送电子邮件至 `sales@impact.com` ，获取无附加条件的演示。以下是迁移流程和最佳实践指南。

#### 迁移角色与职责[](https://help.impact.com/other/zh/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#qian-yi-jue-se-yu-zhi-ze)

角色

职责

**联盟经理或合作伙伴经理**

此角色负责在 Onboarding Migration Specialist 的协助下，将合作伙伴从旧平台迁移到 impact.com。

**代理客户经理或项目经理**

如果您与代理机构合作，通常由代理机构的一名代表代您管理合作伙伴迁移，并担任项目经理。

**impact.com 入驻成功经理**

此角色负责新的账户设置流程，并确保新项目的所有方面都正确配置，包括跟踪设置、产品目录和合作伙伴合同。

**impact.com 实施工程师**

此角色负责技术集成流程，并在必要时直接与您的技术团队联络，以确保跟踪的技术方面运行完美无误。

**网页开发人员或技术联系人**

此角色负责在您的网站上部署 JavaScript，协助进行任何必要的数据传输，测试集成，并确保技术项目按时完成并经过测试。

#### 迁移流程[](https://help.impact.com/other/zh/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#qian-yi-liu-cheng)

impact.com 通常建议尽快通知您的合作伙伴有关过渡的信息。还建议在关闭旧项目和运行新项目之间保留 2-4 周的重叠期，以便为合作伙伴注册和更换链接留出时间。

一旦 impact.com 获得开始迁移的绿灯，将执行以下步骤。

1.  **账户设置**
    
    在此阶段，您将获得一位指定的 impact.com 入驻成功经理，他/她将与您密切合作，配置所有账户设置，包括跟踪、合同和产品目录。
    
2.  **技术账户集成**
    
    同时，impact.com 实施工程师将管理技术集成流程，与网页开发人员或技术联系人合作，确保所有跟踪正常运行、所有参数正确传递，并对一切进行全面测试。
    
3.  **合作伙伴迁移**
    
    现在从技术角度来看，该项目已经准备就绪，合作伙伴可以被迁移过来，并在应用中正式上线。
    
    您的联盟或合作伙伴经理（或您的代理机构）将与您的合作伙伴群体沟通即将进行的迁移。随后，impact.com 的 Onboarding Migration Specialist 将提供迁移合作伙伴群体的最佳实践和消息模板，并会定期跟进，以确保迁移顺利进行。
    
    *   **如果您包含 Optimize：** 最好一次迁移一组合作伙伴，而不是一次性全部迁移。impact.com 在两个平台同时运行期间可确保转化 [去重](https://help.impact.com/brand/zh/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/cross-channel-performance-insights/configure-affiliate-networks-for-deduplication)
         。
        
    

到这里，迁移就完成了。

#### 最佳实践[](https://help.impact.com/other/zh/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#zui-jia-shi-jian)

*   与您现有的网络沟通，告知他们您将迁出，并且今后不再使用他们的服务。
    
    **重要：** 首先检查您当前网络的取消政策，因为合同中可能包含会阻止迁移的条款。
    
*   制定一个现实可行的时间表来促进迁移，并为合作伙伴留出充足时间进行或安排更改。
    
*   考虑合作伙伴的需求，因为有些合作伙伴有集成需求，因此需要提前沟通。
    
*   确保当前网络上的合作伙伴付款已结清，因为有些合作伙伴可能会因为尚有未付款项而不迁移。
    
*   在迁移进展展开时，让合作伙伴及时了解最新情况。若可能，请提供一个电子邮件别名，以便在迁移前筛选任何问题。您还可以创建指南或 [向合作伙伴提供资源](https://help.impact.com/brand/zh/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/ads/requests-from-partners/create-a-resource-to-share-with-partners)
     ，为刚迁移到 impact.com 的合作伙伴提供支持。请查看 ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2499487857-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FhRN1rcrim887TwHLBjac%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=64d9b963966c0dd5e3924d6375c263f9&sv=3) **Engage → Content → Resources** 以获取更多有用的迁移信息。
    

#### 迁移沟通[](https://help.impact.com/other/zh/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#qian-yi-gou-tong)

下面的沟通时间表为完整迁移预留了 6-8 周，但根据合作伙伴数量，这一期限可能更短或更长：

8 周时间表[](https://help.impact.com/other/zh/reference-documentation/migrate-your-performance-or-partner-program-onto-impactcom#id-8-zhou-shi-jian-biao)

上线日期前 2 周

会发送一则即将迁移的通知，其中包括结束日期以及关于 impact.com 的附加信息。应与所有顶级合作伙伴安排电话沟通，亲自通知他们，并在链接可用后（上线后）尽快更换链接。优先事项是让所有顶级合作伙伴完成迁移。请参见 **上线前电子邮件模板** 如下。

impact.com 建议在开始之前先下载完整的合作伙伴名单。这在合作伙伴未能及时迁移的情况下会很有帮助。

来自您先前网络/平台的绩效报告可以帮助优先迁移能带来销售额的合作伙伴。这将作为您的路线图，以尽量减少迁移期间可能出现的销售收入下降。

上线当天

向所有合作伙伴发送电子邮件，其中包含 Public Terms 的直接注册链接或特定模板条款的注册链接（视合作伙伴而定）。重申旧项目的结束日期。

上线后 1 周

应已联系顶级合作伙伴，他们应正在更换链接。impact.com 建议密切监控他们的迁移进度。请参见 **上线后电子邮件模板** 如下。

上线后 2 周

开始优先联系中层合作伙伴。发送另一封沟通信，提醒发布者：在结束日期之后发生的销售将不会在旧平台上计入。如果他们希望继续推广该活动，则需要加入您在 impact.com 上的项目。

迁移结束前 2 周

impact.com 将开始抓取现有合作伙伴网站，查找旧网络的链接。随后，这些合作伙伴可以被加入 impact.com 中的沟通流程，并持续跟进以更新过时链接。

优先跟进仍处于待处理状态的顶级合作伙伴。

迁移结束前 1 周

发送提醒邮件，说明该项目将在 7 天后关闭，并附上您 impact.com 账户注册页面的链接。理想情况下，到这时所有顶级合作伙伴以及大多数中层合作伙伴都应已在 impact.com 上线。

迁移结束前 1 天

发送最终通知，提醒合作伙伴迁移结束日期之后的任何销售都将无效，并附上 impact.com 的注册链接。

迁移完成

所有顶级合作伙伴都已注册，并通过其 impact.com 链接进行跟踪。所有尚未注册的中低层合作伙伴仍可通过市场或您的注册链接托管位置完成注册。

您可以向现有合作伙伴发送两封示例邮件，宣布即将迁移到 impact.com。

**仅限 Deluxe 迁移套餐**：可与 Migration Specialist 讨论定制消息。

示例 1：上线前邮件

示例 2：上线后邮件

这封邮件可发送给您现有的合作伙伴，以宣布即将迁移到 impact.com。

**主题**：需要采取行动： **项目名称** - 更新

您好 `{first name}`,

我们关于合作伙伴项目有一个非常令人振奋的消息！自 `{date links will expire}`, `{ProgramName}` 将完全运行在一个更强大的平台上，并终止我们与 `{PreviousPlatform}`.

的合作关系。此变更将使我们能够为合作伙伴提供更多机会，包括更准确的跟踪、更强大的归因逻辑、增强的报告能力、动态佣金机制以及多种新的广告类型。新的合作伙伴界面还将使您能够跟踪自己的绩效和付款状态。

我们很快会发送一封包含更多详细信息以及新跟踪解决方案注册信息的通知。如果您在接下来的 `{add ETA}` 内没有收到此通知，请直接联系我，邮箱为 `{contact’s email}`.

感谢您在我们进行此次变更时给予的合作与灵活配合，以便为我们的合作伙伴项目提供一个更具可扩展性的解决方案。

谢谢，

这封邮件可发送给您的合作伙伴，以启动迁移流程。

**主题**：需要采取行动： **项目名称** - 更新

您好 `{first name}`,

正如我们之前提到的， `{ProgramName}` 合作伙伴项目正从 `{PreviousPlatform}` 迁移到 impact.com。该项目在 `{PreviousPlatform}` 上的最后可用日期是 `{add date}`。请在那之前迁移您的链接，以确保我们能够继续向您支付佣金。

为此，请按照以下步骤操作：

1.  加入 `{ProgramName}` 项目，注册链接在此 `{add your impact.com sign-up link}`。当您成功注册到我们的新项目后，您将收到一封电子邮件。
    
    *   [登录](https://app.impact.com/login.user)
         您的 impact.com 账户。
        
    *   **创建** 一个链接，使用 _Create and Share Link_ 小组件，位于您的仪表板页面。
        
    
2.  选择 ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2499487857-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FhRN1rcrim887TwHLBjac%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c96dfc646871077ddf2e4cc2ea81175daa3d0531%252F79e45d44813a3f6d7b87329fec56d9ef69c7b18ad8c329528af6456767aaabac.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=55171bbd48bb3ca18478b8851d0b25c3&sv=3) **\[帮助\]** 在右下角可访问 impact.com 帮助中心，或向支持团队提交工单。
    

我们随时准备回答问题，并在需要时帮助您完成过渡流程。

请在您完成迁移后尽快给我们发邮件！

谢谢，

: ACH, transferência bancária e PayPal.

    **Importante:** A impact.com não oferece suporte a novos cadastros de parceiros com a moeda BRL. No entanto, se um parceiro se cadastrou antes da restrição, ele ainda poderá receber pagamentos em BRL.

# Unterstützte Währungen & Zeitzonen | Other | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/other/de/reference-documentation/supported-currencies-and-timezones.md)
.

Sehen Sie sich alle von impact.com unterstützten Währungen und Zeitzonen an.

#### Alle Währungen[](https://help.impact.com/other/de/reference-documentation/supported-currencies-and-timezones#alle-wahrungen)

Die Währungsunterstützung kann je nach Zahlungs- oder Auszahlungsart variieren. Erfahren Sie mehr über Ihre Finanz­einstellungen und Optionen als [Marke](https://help.impact.com/brand/de/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance)
, [Agentur](https://help.impact.com/agency/de/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/finance)
, oder [Partner / Creator](https://help.impact.com/partner/de/was-mochten-sie-lernen/platform-features/finance)
.

#### Währungen nach Auszahlungsart für Partner[](https://help.impact.com/other/de/reference-documentation/supported-currencies-and-timezones#wahrungen-nach-auszahlungsart-fur-partner)

Für Partner sind je nach [Auszahlungsart](https://help.impact.com/partner/de/was-mochten-sie-lernen/platform-features/finance/payments-withdrawals-and-balance/withdraw-funds-to-your-bank-account)
verschiedene Währungen verfügbar: ACH, Überweisung und PayPal.

Unterstützte ACH-Währungen[](https://help.impact.com/other/de/reference-documentation/supported-currencies-and-timezones#unterstutzte-ach-wahrungen)

*   **AUD** - Australischer Dollar
    
*   **BRL\*** - Brasilianischer Real\*
    
    **Wichtig:** impact.com unterstützt keine neuen Partneranmeldungen mit der BRL-Währung. Wenn sich ein Partner jedoch vor der Einschränkung angemeldet hat, kann er weiterhin Auszahlungen in BRL erhalten.

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/other/zh/reference-documentation/supported-currencies-and-timezones.md)
.

查看 impact.com 支持的所有货币和时区。

#### 所有货币[](https://help.impact.com/other/zh/reference-documentation/supported-currencies-and-timezones#suo-you-huo-bi)

货币支持可能会因付款或提现方式而异。了解更多关于您的财务设置和选项，作为 [品牌](https://help.impact.com/brand/zh/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance)
, [代理商](https://help.impact.com/agency/zh/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/finance)
，或 [合作伙伴 / 创作者](https://help.impact.com/partner/zh/nin-xiang-liao-jie-shen-me/platform-features/finance)
.
